{"parentUuid":"1440fbb0-2ab5-42ea-b2c1-98a464e2c2de","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX466hXbopxy9HmpyYHe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HvxoHaw2woF1aRr9oRACjc","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t00.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6467,"cache_read_input_tokens":64670,"output_tokens":428,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":92},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6467,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":428,"cache_read_input_tokens":64670,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6467,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6467},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01HvxoHaw2woF1aRr9oRACjc":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t00.py"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"a34b78c0-968f-472c-9e67-4d2fe066479e","apiBlockIndex":4,"requestId":"req_011CfX466KD1mgMyPfwJDsGZ","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"1af1449b-1478-4dda-b1e8-1d637421c97b","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:53:48.060Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":null,"session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"1c5e896c-d2af-47a7-98a4-f5d411f0ba1e","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"e737f33b-8761-4359-aa76-57233420536a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015fRYMJA9z9VJ2SwsevUFAH","type":"tool_result","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nLa Once Mil (the Michelin-starred flagship taquería at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de\nChapultepec, Mexico City) has no usable web presence. Its official domain\n(`laoncemil.com`) is a permanent \"under construction\" page, and its real online\npresence — Instagram, TikTok, a Linktree — buries the one thing most visitors need\n(address, hours, \"is it walk-in only\") under a scrolling feed. A first-time visitor\ndrawn by Michelin coverage, or a repeat local visitor checking today's hours, has no\nfast way to get that information without digging through a social feed they may not\nalready follow. There is also no simple way for the restaurant to post a dated\nannouncement (e.g. news, seasonal changes) without it disappearing into that same feed.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with a local admin console (never exposed to the internet, per ADR-001)\nand a static public site exported to GitHub Pages. The public Home page leads with\nverified address, hours, a directions link, and a prominent Call button, because that\nis the one glance a visitor needs. A Manager role can publish News, edit the\npermanent pages (Home, Menu, Our Story), and manage user accounts; an Editor role can\ndraft News for the Manager to review and publish, but cannot publish anything, edit\nthe permanent pages, or touch user accounts. Publishing marks content as published in\nthe local CMS only — the public GitHub Pages site reflects that change only after\n`cms publish` exports to `site/` and that export is deployed.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Manager, I want to log in with a hashed password, so that only I can reach\n   the admin console.\n2. As the Manager, I want to log out, so that a shared or public machine doesn't stay\n   signed in.\n3. As anyone entering a wrong password, I want a clear rejection message with no\n   account created or session started, so that login failures are unambiguous.\n4. As the Editor, I want to log in with my own hashed-password account, so that I can\n   draft News without sharing the Manager's credentials.\n5. As an anonymous visitor, I want to be redirected to login when I try to reach any\n   admin route, so that the admin console is never reachable without authentication.\n6. As the Manager, I want the Editor's role to visibly differ from mine (different\n   nav options, different available actions), so that the two roles are genuinely\n   distinct, not just a label.\n7. As the Editor, I want to be blocked (403 or redirect, never a silent no-op) if I\n   submit a direct HTTP request to a Manager-only route (publish, permanent-page edit,\n   user management) even without using the UI, so that the restriction can't be\n   bypassed by hand-crafting a request.\n8. As the Manager, I want to create, edit, and delete News posts (title, slug,\n   Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps), so that I can keep the\n   public site current.\n9. As the Editor, I want to create and edit News posts as drafts, so that I can\n   prepare an announcement for the Manager's review.\n10. As the Editor, I want my save actions to always leave the post in draft status\n    (never published), so that I cannot accidentally or deliberately publish content.\n11. As the Manager, I want to publish or unpublish a News post, so that I control what\n    reaches the public site.\n12. As the Manager, I want the News list to be filterable by status (draft/published),\n    so that I can find what still needs review.\n13. As the Manager, I want to edit the Home, Menu, and Our Story pages (each with\n    title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps, matching\n    the Posts capability's fields), so that I control the site's permanent content.\n14. As the Editor, I want the permanent-page editors to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect if I try the route directly), so that the\n    \"Editor never touches permanent pages\" rule is enforced, not just hidden.\n15. As a visitor, I want a published permanent page to appear in the public site's\n    navigation once it's exported, so that I can reach Menu and Our Story from Home.\n16. As the Manager, I want a dashboard that shows counts of News posts (draft vs.\n    published) and permanent-page status, so that I can see the site's state at a\n    glance.\n17. As the Manager, I want a content list I can filter by type (News/Pages) and by\n    status, so that I can navigate a growing set of content without scrolling\n    everything.\n18. As the Manager or Editor, I want a Markdown preview in the editor before saving,\n    so that I can see how the rendered content will look.\n19. As the Manager, I want to create a new user account (email, password, role), so\n    that I can add an Editor when needed.\n20. As the Manager, I want to change a user's role, so that I can promote or demote\n    without deleting and recreating the account.\n21. As the Manager, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who shouldn't have\n    access anymore loses it without deleting their historical authorship record.\n22. As a deactivated user, I want my login attempt to be refused with a clear message,\n    even with the correct password, so that deactivation is actually enforced.\n23. As the Editor, I want the user-management routes to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect on direct request), so that only the Manager\n    controls accounts.\n24. As a visitor, I want the public Home page to show verified address, hours, a\n    directions link, and a prominent Call button, prioritized above other content\n    (signature dish, walk-in-only note), so that I get the \"where and when\" answer\n    immediately.\n25. As a visitor, I want the public site to show only published content — no draft\n    ever appears — so that I never see unfinished or unreviewed material.\n26. As a visitor, I want any Markdown-authored content (News, Home, Menu, Our Story)\n    to render safely, with scripts and dangerous attributes stripped, so that the\n    public site can't be used to inject malicious content even if a draft author\n    pasted something unsafe.\n27. As a visitor, I want every link and asset on the exported site to use a relative\n    path (never root-absolute), so that the site works correctly when served from a\n    GitHub Pages project subfolder.\n28. As a visitor, I want every published page — including Home — reachable by\n    clicking through the site's navigation, so that no published content is an\n    unlinked orphan.\n29. As the Manager, I want `cms publish` to regenerate `site/` from only the current\n    published content, so that unpublishing something and re-running publish actually\n    removes it from the export.\n30. As the Manager, I want a News post to include one real, dated Michelin star\n    announcement (attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address, matching the\n    Michelin Guide's own attribution) alongside clearly labeled demo announcements, so\n    that the demo doesn't read as entirely fictional while staying honest about what's\n    invented.\n31. As anyone viewing any seed/demo content, I want a clear label distinguishing real,\n    sourced content from invented sample content, so that the site never implies La\n    Once Mil created or endorsed this project.\n32. As the grader/developer, I want two seeded demo accounts (\"Demo Manager\",\n    \"Demo Editor\") that are clearly fictional and not tied to the real owner's\n    identity, so that both roles are testable without misrepresenting real staff.\n33. As the Manager, I want the Menu page to be one ordinary Markdown page listing dish\n    names and descriptions with no prices, so that I never publish an unverified peso\n    price.\n34. As a visitor, I want image placeholders (e.g. `[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`)\n    to render as clearly labeled placeholders rather than broken image tags, so that\n    missing photography doesn't look like a bug.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: two enforced roles, `admin` (\"Manager\" in client-facing copy) and\n  `editor`. Role is a column on the user record, checked server-side on every\n  state-changing and every admin-only read route — never enforced only by hiding UI.\n- **Permission matrix**:\n  - `admin`: create/edit/delete/publish News; create/edit permanent pages (Home,\n    Menu, Our Story); create/edit-role/deactivate users; view dashboard and full\n    content list.\n  - `editor`: create/edit News as drafts only (cannot set status to published, cannot\n    delete a published post); cannot reach permanent-page editors or user-management\n    routes (403/redirect on both UI and direct request); can view the dashboard and\n    content list scoped to what they're allowed to act on.\n- **Content types**: `Post` (News) and `Page` (Home, Menu, Our Story) share the same\n  field set — title, slug, Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author\n  (foreign key to user), created/updated timestamps. `Page` additionally participates\n  in the public navigation once published; `Post` (News) is listed reverse-chronological\n  on a News index.\n- **Pages support the same create/read/update/delete operations as Posts**\n  (corrected 2026-09-29; see the comment on this issue). Home, Menu, and Our Story\n  are La Once Mil's starting content, seeded by `scripts/seed_demo.py`, not a fixed\n  limit — the Manager can create additional Pages and delete any Page, matching the\n  manual/rubric's \"Pages: same fields and operations as posts.\" Home is not a nav\n  entry itself (it's the entry point); Menu and Our Story are.\n- **Publish/export boundary (ADR-001)**: \"published\" is a status flag in the local\n  SQLite database — it does not touch the public site. `cms publish` (via\n  `render_site()` in `app/publish.py`) reads only `status = published` rows, renders\n  Markdown through the sanitizer, and writes `site/` with one file per published item\n  plus a News index and nav links built from published Pages. `cms deploy` pushes\n  `site/` to `gh-pages`. These are three distinct moments (publish-in-CMS, export,\n  deploy) and the spec never conflates them.\n- **Sanitization**: Markdown bodies are converted with `markdown-it-py` then passed\n  through `nh3` (already a pinned dependency) before being written to `site/` or\n  rendered in the admin preview — both surfaces sanitize, not just the export.\n- **Sessions**: signed session cookies (`itsdangerous`, already pinned) carrying user\n  id and role; no client-side role trust — every request re-checks role from the\n  session-resolved user record so a deactivated user's existing session stops working\n  on the next request, not just at next login.\n- **Password storage**: argon2 (`argon2-cffi`, already pinned) hashes only; plaintext\n  is never stored or logged.\n- **CSRF**: every state-changing form (login excluded, since it has no prior session)\n  carries a CSRF token validated server-side before the action runs.\n- **Deactivation**: a boolean/status flag on the user record. Deactivated users fail\n  login with a clear rejection message even with the correct password, and any\n  existing session for that user is treated as invalid on the next authenticated\n  request.\n- **Seed data** (`scripts/seed_demo.py`): creates exactly two users, \"Demo Manager\"\n  (admin) and \"Demo Editor\" (editor), with passwords read from the environment\n  (`.env.example` documents the variable names) — never hard-coded, never the real\n  owner's name. Seeds the three permanent pages (Home/Menu/Our Story, published) and\n  a small set of News posts: one real dated item (the Michelin star announcement,\n  attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address) plus additional clearly-labeled\n  demo announcements (e.g. a `[DEMO CONTENT]` marker in the body or a dedicated\n  \"demo\" flag rendered as a badge).\n- **Images**: no upload/media-library feature in this spec (that's the \"Media uploads\"\n  stretch goal). Placeholder images are literal labeled text/markup\n  (`[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`) embedded in seed content, rendered as visible\n  placeholder blocks, not broken `<img>` tags.\n- **Modules to build** (new, under `app/`): `app/models.py` or `app/db.py` (SQLite\n  schema: users, pages, posts), `app/auth.py` (login/session/CSRF/role-check\n  helpers), `app/routes/auth.py`, `app/routes/posts.py`, `app/routes/pages.py`,\n  `app/routes/users.py`, `app/routes/admin.py` (dashboard/content list), each\n  `include_router`'d from `app/main.py` per its existing comment. `app/publish.py`\n  extends `render_site()` to iterate published Posts/Pages instead of the current\n  hard-coded empty-items placeholder.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- Tests only assert on external behavior — HTTP status/redirect/response body for the\n  HTTP seam, and files/content under `site/` for the export seam. No test reaches into\n  the database or calls an internal function to check state a real user/visitor\n  couldn't observe.\n- **Seam 1 — HTTP** (`client`, `client_as` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py`,\n  prior art: `tests/test_t00.py`): login success/failure/logout; anonymous access to\n  any admin route redirects to login; `client_as(\"editor\")` gets 403/redirect on\n  every admin-only route (permanent-page edit, publish action, user management) —\n  including a direct POST that bypasses the UI entirely, matching the \"editors cannot\n  bypass restrictions by submitting requests directly\" requirement; Editor CRUD on\n  News is confined to draft status; Manager can publish/unpublish; dashboard and\n  content-list filters return the expected counts/subsets; user the next request.\n- **Seam 2 — Export** (`render_site()` in `app/publish.py`, prior art:\n  `tests/test_t00.py`'s two existing publish tests): a draft never appears in any file\n  under `site/`; a `<script>`/`onerror=` payload saved in a post's body does not\n  survive into the exported HTML unescaped/executable; every `href=`/`src=` in the\n  exported output is relative, never root-absolute; removes it from the output.\n- No new test seam is introduced — both existing fixtures/functions are extended, not\n  replaced.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Contact form or any customer-facing email tickets.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0ab03a99-7f14-4c65-afc7-edfac0306e7f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:53:48.473Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"## Problem Statement\n\nLa Once Mil (the Michelin-starred flagship taquería at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de\nChapultepec, Mexico City) has no usable web presence. Its official domain\n(`laoncemil.com`) is a permanent \"under construction\" page, and its real online\npresence — Instagram, TikTok, a Linktree — buries the one thing most visitors need\n(address, hours, \"is it walk-in only\") under a scrolling feed. A first-time visitor\ndrawn by Michelin coverage, or a repeat local visitor checking today's hours, has no\nfast way to get that information without digging through a social feed they may not\nalready follow. There is also no simple way for the restaurant to post a dated\nannouncement (e.g. news, seasonal changes) without it disappearing into that same feed.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with a local admin console (never exposed to the internet, per ADR-001)\nand a static public site exported to GitHub Pages. The public Home page leads with\nverified address, hours, a directions link, and a prominent Call button, because that\nis the one glance a visitor needs. A Manager role can publish News, edit the\npermanent pages (Home, Menu, Our Story), and manage user accounts; an Editor role can\ndraft News for the Manager to review and publish, but cannot publish anything, edit\nthe permanent pages, or touch user accounts. Publishing marks content as published in\nthe local CMS only — the public GitHub Pages site reflects that change only after\n`cms publish` exports to `site/` and that export is deployed.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Manager, I want to log in with a hashed password, so that only I can reach\n   the admin console.\n2. As the Manager, I want to log out, so that a shared or public machine doesn't stay\n   signed in.\n3. As anyone entering a wrong password, I want a clear rejection message with no\n   account created or session started, so that login failures are unambiguous.\n4. As the Editor, I want to log in with my own hashed-password account, so that I can\n   draft News without sharing the Manager's credentials.\n5. As an anonymous visitor, I want to be redirected to login when I try to reach any\n   admin route, so that the admin console is never reachable without authentication.\n6. As the Manager, I want the Editor's role to visibly differ from mine (different\n   nav options, different available actions), so that the two roles are genuinely\n   distinct, not just a label.\n7. As the Editor, I want to be blocked (403 or redirect, never a silent no-op) if I\n   submit a direct HTTP request to a Manager-only route (publish, permanent-page edit,\n   user management) even without using the UI, so that the restriction can't be\n   bypassed by hand-crafting a request.\n8. As the Manager, I want to create, edit, and delete News posts (title, slug,\n   Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps), so that I can keep the\n   public site current.\n9. As the Editor, I want to create and edit News posts as drafts, so that I can\n   prepare an announcement for the Manager's review.\n10. As the Editor, I want my save actions to always leave the post in draft status\n    (never published), so that I cannot accidentally or deliberately publish content.\n11. As the Manager, I want to publish or unpublish a News post, so that I control what\n    reaches the public site.\n12. As the Manager, I want the News list to be filterable by status (draft/published),\n    so that I can find what still needs review.\n13. As the Manager, I want to edit the Home, Menu, and Our Story pages (each with\n    title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps, matching\n    the Posts capability's fields), so that I control the site's permanent content.\n14. As the Editor, I want the permanent-page editors to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect if I try the route directly), so that the\n    \"Editor never touches permanent pages\" rule is enforced, not just hidden.\n15. As a visitor, I want a published permanent page to appear in the public site's\n    navigation once it's exported, so that I can reach Menu and Our Story from Home.\n16. As the Manager, I want a dashboard that shows counts of News posts (draft vs.\n    published) and permanent-page status, so that I can see the site's state at a\n    glance.\n17. As the Manager, I want a content list I can filter by type (News/Pages) and by\n    status, so that I can navigate a growing set of content without scrolling\n    everything.\n18. As the Manager or Editor, I want a Markdown preview in the editor before saving,\n    so that I can see how the rendered content will look.\n19. As the Manager, I want to create a new user account (email, password, role), so\n    that I can add an Editor when needed.\n20. As the Manager, I want to change a user's role, so that I can promote or demote\n    without deleting and recreating the account.\n21. As the Manager, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who shouldn't have\n    access anymore loses it without deleting their historical authorship record.\n22. As a deactivated user, I want my login attempt to be refused with a clear message,\n    even with the correct password, so that deactivation is actually enforced.\n23. As the Editor, I want the user-management routes to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect on direct request), so that only the Manager\n    controls accounts.\n24. As a visitor, I want the public Home page to show verified address, hours, a\n    directions link, and a prominent Call button, prioritized above other content\n    (signature dish, walk-in-only note), so that I get the \"where and when\" answer\n    immediately.\n25. As a visitor, I want the public site to show only published content — no draft\n    ever appears — so that I never see unfinished or unreviewed material.\n26. As a visitor, I want any Markdown-authored content (News, Home, Menu, Our Story)\n    to render safely, with scripts and dangerous attributes stripped, so that the\n    public site can't be used to inject malicious content even if a draft author\n    pasted something unsafe.\n27. As a visitor, I want every link and asset on the exported site to use a relative\n    path (never root-absolute), so that the site works correctly when served from a\n    GitHub Pages project subfolder.\n28. As a visitor, I want every published page — including Home — reachable by\n    clicking through the site's navigation, so that no published content is an\n    unlinked orphan.\n29. As the Manager, I want `cms publish` to regenerate `site/` from only the current\n    published content, so that unpublishing something and re-running publish actually\n    removes it from the export.\n30. As the Manager, I want a News post to include one real, dated Michelin star\n    announcement (attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address, matching the\n    Michelin Guide's own attribution) alongside clearly labeled demo announcements, so\n    that the demo doesn't read as entirely fictional while staying honest about what's\n    invented.\n31. As anyone viewing any seed/demo content, I want a clear label distinguishing real,\n    sourced content from invented sample content, so that the site never implies La\n    Once Mil created or endorsed this project.\n32. As the grader/developer, I want two seeded demo accounts (\"Demo Manager\",\n    \"Demo Editor\") that are clearly fictional and not tied to the real owner's\n    identity, so that both roles are testable without misrepresenting real staff.\n33. As the Manager, I want the Menu page to be one ordinary Markdown page listing dish\n    names and descriptions with no prices, so that I never publish an unverified peso\n    price.\n34. As a visitor, I want image placeholders (e.g. `[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`)\n    to render as clearly labeled placeholders rather than broken image tags, so that\n    missing photography doesn't look like a bug.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: two enforced roles, `admin` (\"Manager\" in client-facing copy) and\n  `editor`. Role is a column on the user record, checked server-side on every\n  state-changing and every admin-only read route — never enforced only by hiding UI.\n- **Permission matrix**:\n  - `admin`: create/edit/delete/publish News; create/edit permanent pages (Home,\n    Menu, Our Story); create/edit-role/deactivate users; view dashboard and full\n    content list.\n  - `editor`: create/edit News as drafts only (cannot set status to published, cannot\n    delete a published post); cannot reach permanent-page editors or user-management\n    routes (403/redirect on both UI and direct request); can view the dashboard and\n    content list scoped to what they're allowed to act on.\n- **Content types**: `Post` (News) and `Page` (Home, Menu, Our Story) share the same\n  field set — title, slug, Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author\n  (foreign key to user), created/updated timestamps. `Page` additionally participates\n  in the public navigation once published; `Post` (News) is listed reverse-chronological\n  on a News index.\n- **Pages support the same create/read/update/delete operations as Posts**\n  (corrected 2026-09-29; see the comment on this issue). Home, Menu, and Our Story\n  are La Once Mil's starting content, seeded by `scripts/seed_demo.py`, not a fixed\n  limit — the Manager can create additional Pages and delete any Page, matching the\n  manual/rubric's \"Pages: same fields and operations as posts.\" Home is not a nav\n  entry itself (it's the entry point); Menu and Our Story are.\n- **Publish/export boundary (ADR-001)**: \"published\" is a status flag in the local\n  SQLite database — it does not touch the public site. `cms publish` (via\n  `render_site()` in `app/publish.py`) reads only `status = published` rows, renders\n  Markdown through the sanitizer, and writes `site/` with one file per published item\n  plus a News index and nav links built from published Pages. `cms deploy` pushes\n  `site/` to `gh-pages`. These are three distinct moments (publish-in-CMS, export,\n  deploy) and the spec never conflates them.\n- **Sanitization**: Markdown bodies are converted with `markdown-it-py` then passed\n  through `nh3` (already a pinned dependency) before being written to `site/` or\n  rendered in the admin preview — both surfaces sanitize, not just the export.\n- **Sessions**: signed session cookies (`itsdangerous`, already pinned) carrying user\n  id and role; no client-side role trust — every request re-checks role from the\n  session-resolved user record so a deactivated user's existing session stops working\n  on the next request, not just at next login.\n- **Password storage**: argon2 (`argon2-cffi`, already pinned) hashes only; plaintext\n  is never stored or logged.\n- **CSRF**: every state-changing form (login excluded, since it has no prior session)\n  carries a CSRF token validated server-side before the action runs.\n- **Deactivation**: a boolean/status flag on the user record. Deactivated users fail\n  login with a clear rejection message even with the correct password, and any\n  existing session for that user is treated as invalid on the next authenticated\n  request.\n- **Seed data** (`scripts/seed_demo.py`): creates exactly two users, \"Demo Manager\"\n  (admin) and \"Demo Editor\" (editor), with passwords read from the environment\n  (`.env.example` documents the variable names) — never hard-coded, never the real\n  owner's name. Seeds the three permanent pages (Home/Menu/Our Story, published) and\n  a small set of News posts: one real dated item (the Michelin star announcement,\n  attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address) plus additional clearly-labeled\n  demo announcements (e.g. a `[DEMO CONTENT]` marker in the body or a dedicated\n  \"demo\" flag rendered as a badge).\n- **Images**: no upload/media-library feature in this spec (that's the \"Media uploads\"\n  stretch goal). Placeholder images are literal labeled text/markup\n  (`[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`) embedded in seed content, rendered as visible\n  placeholder blocks, not broken `<img>` tags.\n- **Modules to build** (new, under `app/`): `app/models.py` or `app/db.py` (SQLite\n  schema: users, pages, posts), `app/auth.py` (login/session/CSRF/role-check\n  helpers), `app/routes/auth.py`, `app/routes/posts.py`, `app/routes/pages.py`,\n  `app/routes/users.py`, `app/routes/admin.py` (dashboard/content list), each\n  `include_router`'d from `app/main.py` per its existing comment. `app/publish.py`\n  extends `render_site()` to iterate published Posts/Pages instead of the current\n  hard-coded empty-items placeholder.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- Tests only assert on external behavior — HTTP status/redirect/response body for the\n  HTTP seam, and files/content under `site/` for the export seam. No test reaches into\n  the database or calls an internal function to check state a real user/visitor\n  couldn't observe.\n- **Seam 1 — HTTP** (`client`, `client_as` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py`,\n  prior art: `tests/test_t00.py`): login success/failure/logout; anonymous access to\n  any admin route redirects to login; `client_as(\"editor\")` gets 403/redirect on\n  every admin-only route (permanent-page edit, publish action, user management) —\n  including a direct POST that bypasses the UI entirely, matching the \"editors cannot\n  bypass restrictions by submitting {"parentUuid":"5234f522-9081-4bb9-aa67-93fd3ac0f2d0","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"e737f33b-8761-4359-aa76-57233420536a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A5Jmgq2GfwvjRWWb9rXRk3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"T02: Pages (create/read/update/delete), seeded with Home, Menu, Our Story.\n2\t\n3\tPer the MP2 rubric/manual, Pages support the same CRUD operations as Posts;\n4\tHome/Menu/Our Story are La Once Mil's starting content, not a hard limit.\n5\tCovers a Manager creating, editing, and deleting a page, an Editor blocked on\n6\tevery route (UI and a hand-crafted direct POST/DELETE), an anonymous visitor\n7\tredirected to login, and Markdown sanitization of a script/onerror payload.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport sqlite3\n12\t\n13\tfrom app import db\n14\t\n15\t\n16\tdef _csrf(client, path):\n17\t    response = client.get(path)\n18\t    assert response.status_code == 200\n19\t    return response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tdef test_manager_can_edit_a_permanent_page(client_as):\n23\t    manager = client_as(\"admin\")\n24\t    csrf = _csrf(manager, \"/admin/pages/home\")\n25\t    response = manager.post(\n26\t        \"/admin/pages/home\",\n27\t        data={\n28\t            \"title\": \"Home\",\n29\t            \"body_md\": \"Visit us at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de Chapultepec.\",\n30\t            \"status\": \"published\",\n31\t            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n32\t        },\n33\t    )\n34\t    assert response.status_code == 200\n35\t    page = db.get_page_by_slug(\"home\")\n36\t    assert page[\"status\"] == \"published\"\n37\t    assert \"Monte Everest 780\" in page[\"body_md\"]\n38\t    assert page[\"author_id\"] is not None\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef test_editor_is_blocked_from_edit_form(client_as):\n42\t    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n43\t    response = editor.get(\"/admin/pages/home\", follow_redirects=False)\n44\t    assert response.status_code == 403\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef test_editor_is_blocked_from_pages_list(client_as):\n48\t    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n49\t    response = editor.get(\"/admin/pages\", follow_redirects=False)\n50\t    assert response.status_code == 403\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef test_editor_is_blocked_from_a_direct_post(client_as):\n54\t    \"\"\"A forged POST with no prior UI visit must still be rejected by role,\n55\t    not merely by a missing CSRF token.\"\"\"\n56\t    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n57\t    response = editor.post(\n58\t        \"/admin/pages/home\",\n59\t        data={\"title\": \"Hijacked\", \"body_md\": \"x\", \"status\": \"published\",\n60\t              \"csrf_token\": \"not-even-a-real-token\"},\n61\t        follow_redirects=False,\n62\t    )\n63\t    assert response.status_code == 403\n64\t    assert db.get_page_by_slug(\"home\")[\"title\"] != \"Hijacked\"\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef test_anonymous_is_redirected_to_login(client):\n68\t    response = client.get(\"/admin/pages/home\", follow_redirects=False)\n69\t    assert response.status_code == 303\n70\t    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n71\t\n72\t    response = client.post(\n73\t        \"/admin/pages/home\",\n74\t        data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"x\", \"status\": \"draft\"},\n75\t        follow_redirects=False,\n76\t    )\n77\t    assert Pages (create/read/update/delete), seeded with Home, Menu, Our Story.\n\nPer the MP2 rubric/manual, Pages support the same CRUD operations as Posts;\nHome/Menu/Our Story are La Once Mil's starting content, not a hard limit.\nCovers a Manager creating, editing, and deleting a page, an Editor blocked on\nevery route (UI and a hand-crafted direct POST/DELETE), an anonymous visitor\nredirected to login, and Markdown sanitization of a script/onerror payload.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\n\n\ndef _csrf(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path)\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n\n\ndef test_manager_can_edit_a_permanent_page(client_as):\n    manager = client_as(\"admin\")\n    csrf = _csrf(manager, \"/admin/pages/home\")\n    response = manager.post(\n        \"/admin/pages/home\",\n        data={\n            \"title\": \"Home\",\n            \"body_md\": \"Visit us at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de Chapultepec.\",\n            \"status\": \"published\",\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(\"home\")\n    assert page[\"status\"] == \"published\"\n    assert \"Monte Everest 780\" in page[\"body_md\"]\n    assert page[\"author_id\"] is not None\n\n\ndef test_editor_is_blocked_from_edit_form(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = editor.get(\"/admin/pages/home\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_editor_is_blocked_from_a_direct_post(client_as):\n    \"\"\"A forged POST with no prior UI visit must still be rejected by role,\n    not merely by a missing 'published')\"\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n    existing_pages_sql = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'pages'\"\n    ).fetchone()[0]\n\n    # Reproduce the migration's steps up to (not including) its final COMMIT,\n    # then simulate a crash by closing the connection without committing.\n    conn.isolation_level = None\n    conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n    conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE pages RENAME TO pages_legacy\")\n    conn.execute(db.PAGES_SCHEMA)\n    conn.execute(\n        f\"INSERT INTO pages ({db._PAGE_COLUMNS}) SELECT {db._PAGE_COLUMNS} FROM pages_legacy\"\n    )\n    conn.execute(\"DROP TABLE pages_legacy\")\n    conn.close()  # crash: no COMMIT\n\n    reopened = sqlite3.connect(legacy_path)\n    reopened.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    home = reopened.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE slug = 'home'\").fetchone()\n    assert home is not None, \"the original row must survive an uncommitted crash\"\n    assert home[\"body_md\"] == \"Existing verified content\"\n    assert \"CHECK (slug IN\" in existing_pages_sql  # sanity check on the fixture itself\n    reopened.close()\n","numLines":325,"startLine":1,"totalLines":325}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5234f522-9081-4bb9-aa67-93fd3ac0f2d0","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    from app.routes import auth, pages\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(pages.router)\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n","numLines":47,"startLine":1,"totalLines":47}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0bee4355-1fca-4d2c-8ff3-a914558d7c4a","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

Posts.\n8\t\n9\tRoute order matters: /admin/pages/new must be declared before the\n10\t/admin/pages/{slug} routes, or FastAPI would match \"new\" as a slug.\n11\t\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\n15\tfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\n16\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n17\t\n18\tfrom app import auth, content, db, settings\n19\t\n20\trouter = APIRouter()\n21\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef _require_manager(request: Request):\n25\t    \"\"\"(user, None) if the caller may manage pages, else (None, response).\"\"\"\n26\t    user = auth.current_user(request)\n27\t    if user is None:\n28\t        return None, RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n29\t    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n30\t        return None, PlainTextResponse(\"Forbidden.\", status_code=403)\n31\t    return user, None\n32\t\n33\t\n34\t@router.get(\"/admin/pages\")\n35\tdef pages_list(request: Request):\n36\t    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n37\t    if error is not None:\n38\t        return error\n39\t    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n40\t        templates, request, \"admin/pages_list.html\",\n41\t        {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"pages\": db.list_pages()},\n42\t editing: create, read, update, delete.\n\nManager (admin) only — the Editor must be blocked in code, not just by hiding\nthe link, on every route here, including a hand-crafted POST. Home, Menu, and\nOur Story are La Once Mil's starting content (see db.PAGE_SLUGS), not a limit:\nper the MP2 rubric/manual, Pages support the same create/read/update/delete\noperations as Posts.\n\nRoute order matters: /admin/pages/new must be declared before the\n/admin/pages/{slug} routes, or FastAPI would match \"new\" as a slug.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, content, db, settings\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef _require_manager(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"(user, None) if the caller may manage pages, else (None, response).\"\"\"\n    user = auth.current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return None, RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        return None, PlainTextResponse(\"Forbidden.\", status_code=403)\n    return user, None\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages\")\ndef pages_list(request: Request):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/pages_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"pages\": db.list_pages()},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages/new\")\ndef page_new_form(request: Request):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"New page\", \"error\": None,\n         \"values\": {\"slug\": \"\", \"title\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\", \"status\": \"draft\"}},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/new\")\ndef page_new_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    slug: str = Form(...),\n    title: str = Form(...),\n    body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n    status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    if status not in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        status = \"draft\"\n\n    values = {\"slug\": slug, \"title\": title, \"body_md\": body_md, \"status\": status}\n\n    if not db.slug_is_valid(slug):\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New page\", \"values\": values,\n             \"error\": \"Slug must be lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.\"},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n\n    created = db.create_page(\n        slug, title=title,\n        body_md=content.sanitize_markdown_source(body_md),\n        status=status, author_id=user[\"id\"],\n    )\n    if not created:\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New page\", \"values\": values,\n             \"error\": f'A page with the slug \"{slug}\" already exists.'},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{slug}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages/{slug}\")\ndef page_edit_form(request: Request, slug: str):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    if page is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": f\"Edit {page['title']}\", \"page\": page,\n         \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": False},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/{slug}\")\ndef page_edit_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    slug: str,\n    title: str = Form(...),\n    body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n    status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if db.get_page_by_slug(slug) is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    if status not in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        status = \"draft\"\n\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=title,\n        body_md=content.sanitize_markdown_source(body_md),\n        status=status,\n        author_id=user[\"id\"],\n    )\n\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": f\"Edit {page['title']}\", \"page\": page,\n         \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": True},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/{slug}/delete\")\ndef page_delete(request: Request, slug: str, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if db.get_page_by_slug(slug) is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n\n    db.delete_page(slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/pages\", status_code=303)\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"68729435-cc81-4472-a8bd-758e3f69b2ec","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"1bb7788c-fc82-4e5e-ac5d-2346e846c07f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX4Bwck7wr33e7LgCBAR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EpP4YjgifzNNKan2F6MTTV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_publish.py","content":"\"\"\"T03: publish pipeline. Extends the T00 publish tests (tests/test_t00.py) to\ncover real Page content: draft exclusion, relative paths, and nav reachability.\n\nSeam 2 per spec #1: render_site() reads only status='published' rows and\nwrites site/ with one file per published Page. No test reaches into the\ndatabase to check anything a visitor couldn't observe by reading the\nexported HTML.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef _set_page(slug, *, status, body_md=None):\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=page[\"title\"],\n        body_md=body_md if body_md is not None else f\"Content for {slug}.\",\n        status=status,\n        author_id=None,\n    )\n\n\ndef _publish_all():\n    for slug in db.PAGE_SLUGS:\n        _set_page(slug, status=\"published\")\n\n\ndef test_home_is_rendered_from_real_page_content(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for home\" in html\n\n\ndef test_other_published_pages_are_written(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert \"Content for menu\" in (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for our-story\" in (out / \"our-story.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_draft_page_is_never_written_to_site(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert \"Content for menu\" not in f.read_text()\n\n\ndef test_all_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        html = f.read_text()\n        assert 'href=\"/' not in html\n        assert 'src=\"/' not in html\n\n\ndef test_every_published_page_is_reachable_from_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    home_html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', home_html))\n    assert {\"menu.html\", \"our-story.html\"} <= hrefs\n\n\ndef test_non_home_pages_link_back_to_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    menu_html = (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"index.html\"' in menu_html\n\n\ndef test_a_page_in_draft_is_excluded_but_others_still_publish(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Acceptance criterion: publishing with one page still in draft.\"\"\"\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"our-story.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_unpublishing_then_republishing_removes_it_from_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out_dir = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    render_site(out_dir)\n    assert (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    render_site(out_dir)\n\n    assert not (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_markdown_is_sanitized_in_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\n        \"home\", status=\"published\",\n        body_md='<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=\"alert(2)\">Tacos',\n    )\n    _publish_all_except_home_body_set = True  # noqa: F841  (readability marker)\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"<script\" not in html\n    assert \"onerror\" not in html\n    assert \"Tacos\" in html\n\n\ndef test_a_freshly_created_extra_page_is_reachable_once_published(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Pages aren't limited to the seeded three (see CONTEXT.md).\"\"\"\n    _publish_all()\n    db.create_page(\n        \"events\", title=\"Events\", body_md=\"Coming soon.\",\n        status=\"published\", author_id=None,\n    )\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"events.html\").exists()\n    home_hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', (out / \"index.html\").read_text()))\n    assert \"events.html\" in home_hrefs\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5673,"cache_read_input_tokens":90507,"output_tokens":1808,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5673,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1808,"cache_read_input_tokens":90507,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5673,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5673},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01EpP4YjgifzNNKan2F6MTTV":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_publish.py","content":"\"\"\"T03: Extends the T00 publish tests (tests/test_t00.py) to\ncover real Page content: draft exclusion, relative paths, and nav reachability.\n\nSeam 2 per spec #1: render_site() reads only status='published' rows and\nwrites site/ with one file per published Page. No test reaches into the\ndatabase to check anything a visitor couldn't observe by reading the\nexported HTML.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef _set_page(slug, *, status, body_md=None):\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=page[\"title\"],\n        body_md=body_md if body_md is not None else f\"Content for {slug}.\",\n        status=status,\n        author_id=None,\n    )\n\n\ndef _publish_all():\n    for slug in db.PAGE_SLUGS:\n        _set_page(slug, status=\"published\")\n\n\ndef test_home_is_rendered_from_real_page_content(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for home\" in html\n\n\ndef test_other_published_pages_are_written(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert \"Content for menu\" in (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for our-story\" in (out / \"our-story.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_draft_page_is_never_written_to_site(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert \"Content for menu\" not in f.read_text()\n\n\ndef test_all_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        html = f.read_text()\n        assert 'href=\"/' not in html\n        assert 'src=\"/' not in html\n\n\ndef test_every_published_page_is_reachable_from_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    home_html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', home_html))\n    assert {\"menu.html\", \"our-story.html\"} <= hrefs\n\n\ndef test_non_home_pages_link_back_to_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    menu_html = (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"index.html\"' in menu_html\n\n\ndef test_a_page_in_draft_is_excluded_but_others_still_publish(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Acceptance criterion: publishing with one page still in draft.\"\"\"\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"our-story.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_unpublishing_then_republishing_removes_it_from_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out_dir = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    render_site(out_dir)\n    assert (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    render_site(out_dir)\n\n    assert not (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_markdown_is_sanitized_in_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\n        \"home\", status=\"published\",\n        body_md='<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=\"alert(2)\">Tacos',\n    )\n    _publish_all_except_home_body_set = True  # noqa: F841  (readability marker)\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"<script\" not in html\n    assert \"onerror\" not in html\n    assert \"Tacos\" in html\n\n\ndef test_a_freshly_created_extra_page_is_reachable_once_published(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Pages aren't limited to the seeded three (see CONTEXT.md).\"\"\"\n    _publish_all()\n    db.create_page(\n        \"events\", title=\"Events\", body_md=\"Coming soon.\",\n        status=\"published\", author_id=None,\n    )\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"events.html\").exists()\n    home_hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', (out / \"index.html\").read_text()))\n    assert \"events.html\" in {"parentUuid":"c4333488-d867-4cbc-a6f2-46fad81e6887","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX4Do4MyFZeGfNqvv8de","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NCq1P9YadH5iG1bgNmKy7k","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/publish.py","old_string":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out","new_string":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nrender_site() reads only published Pages from the database (see CONTEXT.md's\n\"Publish\" entry — published-in-the-CMS is not the same as exported) and\nwrites one file per page: the \"home\" slug becomes index.html, every other\npublished slug becomes \"<slug>.html\". Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"menu.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\nAll exported files live flat at the root of site/, so a single relative name\n(\"style.css\", \"index.html\", \"<slug>.html\") resolves correctly from every page\nwithout tracking directory depth.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import content, db, settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nheader nav ul { display: flex; gap: 1rem; list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0.5rem 0 0; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\nHOME_SLUG = \"home\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _out_name(slug: str) -> str:\n    return \"index.html\" if slug == HOME_SLUG else f\"{slug}.html\"\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    published = [p for p in db.list_pages() if p[\"status\"] == \"published\"]\n\n    # Home is the entry point, not a nav link itself (see CONTEXT.md's \"Pages\"\n    # entry); every other published page appears in the nav on every page.\n    nav_links = [\n As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out","new_string":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nrender_site() reads only published Pages from the database (see CONTEXT.md's\n\"Publish\" entry — published-in-the-CMS is not the same as exported) and\nwrites one file per page: the \"home\" slug becomes index.html, every other\npublished slug becomes \"<slug>.html\". Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"menu.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\nAll exported files live flat at the root of site/, so a single relative name\n(\"style.css\", \"index.html\", \"<slug>.html\") resolves correctly from every page\nwithout tracking directory depth.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import content, db, settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nheader nav ul { display: flex; gap: 1rem; list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0.5rem 0 0; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\nHOME_SLUG = \"home\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _out_name(slug: str) -> str:\n    return \"index.html\" if slug == HOME_SLUG else f\"{slug}.html\"\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    published = [p for p in db.list_pages() if p[\"status\"] == \"published\"]\n\n    # Home is the entry point, not a nav link itself (see CONTEXT.md's \"Pages\"\n    # entry); every other published page appears in the nav on every page.\n    nav_links = [\n        {\"href\": _out_name(p[\"slug\"]), \"title\": p[\"title\"]}\n        for p in published if p[\"slug\"] != HOME_SLUG\n    ]\n\n    template = env.get_template(\"public/page.html\")\n    for page in published:\n        (out / _out_name(page[\"slug\"])).write_text(\n            template.render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE,\n                page_title=page[\"title\"],\n                body_html=content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]),\n                css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\",\n                nav_links=nav_links,\n            )\n        )\n\n    if not any(p[\"slug\"] == HOME_SLUG for p in published):\n        (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\",\n            )\n        )\n\n    return out"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"9f35b2c9-88a2-4609-ba3c-48b5cb07291e","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfX4DndJkiChkQtCx8Cvx","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"318d5775-2a06-426a-b985-61541c34aec3","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:55:41.837Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":null,"session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

Extends the T00 publish tests (tests/test_t00.py) to\ncover real Page content: draft exclusion, relative paths, and nav reachability.\n\nSeam 2 per spec #1: render_site() reads only status='published' rows and\nwrites site/ with one file per published Page. No test reaches into the\ndatabase to check anything a visitor couldn't observe by reading the\nexported HTML.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef _set_page(slug, *, status, body_md=None):\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=page[\"title\"],\n        body_md=body_md if body_md is not None else f\"Content for {slug}.\",\n        status=status,\n        author_id=None,\n    )\n\n\ndef _publish_all():\n    for slug in db.PAGE_SLUGS:\n        _set_page(slug, status=\"published\")\n\n\ndef test_home_is_rendered_from_real_page_content(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for home\" in html\n\n\ndef test_other_published_pages_are_written(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert \"Content for menu\" in (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for our-story\" in (out / \"our-story.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_draft_page_is_never_written_to_site(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert \"Content for menu\" not in f.read_text()\n\n\ndef test_all_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        html = f.read_text()\n        assert 'href=\"/' not in html\n        assert 'src=\"/' not in html\n\n\ndef test_every_published_page_is_reachable_from_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    home_html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', home_html))\n    assert {\"menu.html\", \"our-story.html\"} <= hrefs\n\n\ndef test_non_home_pages_link_back_to_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    menu_html = (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"index.html\"' in menu_html\n\n\ndef test_a_page_in_draft_is_excluded_but_others_still_publish(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Acceptance criterion: publishing with one page still in draft.\"\"\"\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"our-story.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_unpublishing_then_republishing_removes_it_from_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out_dir = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    render_site(out_dir)\n    assert (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    render_site(out_dir)\n\n    assert not (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_markdown_is_sanitized_in_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\n        \"home\", status=\"published\",\n        body_md='<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=\"alert(2)\">Tacos',\n    )\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"<script\" not in html\n    assert \"onerror\" not in html\n    assert \"Tacos\" in html\n\n\ndef test_a_freshly_created_extra_page_is_reachable_once_published(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Pages aren't limited to the seeded three (see CONTEXT.md).\"\"\"\n    _publish_all()\n    db.create_page(\n        \"events\", title=\"Events\", body_md=\"Coming soon.\",\n        status=\"published\", author_id=None,\n    )\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert (out / \"events.html\").exists()\n    home_hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', (out / The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I have enough to finalize. This is a small diff, so a single confirmed finding plus a couple of plausible/cleanup items suffice.\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"file\": \"app/publish.py\",\n    \"line\": 44,\n    \"summary\": \"A published Page whose slug is literally \\\"index\\\" collides with the Home page's output file, silently overwriting index.html.\",\n    \"failure_scenario\": \"Pages have full CRUD (T02) with no reserved-slug list beyond the special-cased \\\"home\\\" (app/db.py's slug_is_valid only checks the regex pattern). If a Manager creates and publishes a page with slug \\\"index\\\", db.list_pages() (ORDER BY slug) yields \\\"home\\\" before \\\"index\\\", so render_site()'s loop writes out/index.html for Home first, then overwrites it with the \\\"index\\\" page's content via the same _out_name(\\\"index\\\") == \\\"index.html\\\" mapping — the published site's homepage is silently replaced by whatever page happened to be named \\\"index\\\", with no error or warning.\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"file\": \"app/publish.py\",\n    \"line\": 77,\n    \"summary\": \"Home fallback silently reverts to the placeholder home.html template if the \\\"home\\\" page is merely a draft (not deleted), discarding the real home content link from nav.\",\n    \"failure_scenario\": \"If a Manager unpublishes (drafts) the Home page while other pages stay published, render_site() falls back to rendering public/home.html with items=[] (\\\"Nothing published yet\\\"), but nav_links (built from published non-home pages) is never passed to that template, so the placeholder home page shows no navigation to Menu/Our Story even though they're published and linked from every other page — the site becomes only reachable from those other pages' back-link, not from the entry point.\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\nFindings summary for the caller:\n\n- **`app/publish.py:44`** (confirmed) — `_out_name()` maps slug `\"home\"` → `index.html`, but any other page whose slug happens to be `\"index\"` also maps to `index.html` (`f\"{slug}.html\"`). Since Pages support full create/delete with slugs validated only by a generic regex (`app/db.py:200`, `slug_is_valid`), nothing stops a Manager from creating and publishing a page slugged `\"index\"`. `db.list_pages()` orders alphabetically, so `\"index\"` is processed after `\"home\"` in the `render_site()` loop and silently overwrites the real homepage file.\n- **`app/publish.py:77`** (plausible) — when no published page has slug `\"home\"` (e.g., Home was drafted rather than deleted), the code falls back to the old placeholder `home.html` template, but that branch doesn't pass `nav_links`, so the fallback homepage loses navigation to the still-published Menu/Our Story pages, weakening the \"every published page reachable from home\" guarantee the new tests otherwise assert.\n\nOther angles (removed-behavior, cross-file callers, reuse/simplification/efficiency, altitude, CLAUDE.md conventions) turned up nothing else that survived verification — the diff correctly filters to `status='published'`, keeps paths relative, and the CSS/nav additions are straightforward with no duplication worth flagging.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>57831</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>15</tool_uses><duration_ms>81037</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}
