See ADR-002 for why\nthere is no ORM here.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import auth, settings\n\n# La Once Mil's initial Pages (see notes/restaurant-research.md); this is\n# seed content, not a limit. Per the MP2 rubric/manual (\"Pages: same fields\n# and operations as posts, plus public navigation\"), Pages support full\n# create/read/update/delete like Posts — the Manager can add or remove pages\n# beyond this starting set.\nPAGE_SLUGS = (\"home\", \"menu\", \"our-story\")\nPAGE_TITLES = {\"home\": \"Home\", \"menu\": \"Menu\", \"our-story\": \"Our Story\"}\n\nUSERS_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\"\"\"\n\nPAGES_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    title TEXT NOT NULL,\n    body_md TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')) DEFAULT 'draft',\n    author_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\"\"\"\n\nSCHEMA = USERS_SCHEMA + PAGES_SCHEMA\n\nSLUG_PATTERN = \"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\"\n\n_PAGE_COLUMNS = \"id, slug, title, body_md, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at\"\n\n\ndef _migrate_legacy_page_slug_check(conn: sqlite3.Connection, existing_pages_sql: str | None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"A database created before Pages supported full CRUD has\n    `CHECK (slug IN ('home', 'menu', 'our-story'))` baked into the `pages`\n    table — `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` never touches an existing table, so\n    that CHECK would otherwise survive forever and silently block creating\n    any other page. Rebuild the table from PAGES_SCHEMA, keeping every row.\n\n    This runs as one explicit transaction (isolation_level=None plus our own\n    BEGIN/COMMIT) rather than relying on the connection's default autocommit\n    handling or `executescript()`: `executescript()` implicitly commits\n    pending work before it runs, and under the default isolation level a DDL\n    statement like ALTER/CREATE/DROP TABLE can commit independently of a\n    later `conn.commit()`. Either would let a crash between steps (process\n    killed, power loss) orphan the renamed table with real content in it\n    while the app reads a freshly emptied `pages` table — confirmed with a\n    crash simulation (kill before the final commit, reopen, check for data\n    loss) before writing it this way.\n    \"\"\"\n    if existing_pages_sql is None or \"CHECK (slug IN\" not in existing_pages_sql:\n        return\n\n    previous_isolation_level = conn.isolation_level\n    conn.isolation_level = None\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE\")\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE pages RENAME TO pages_legacy\")\n        conn.execute(PAGES_SCHEMA)\n        conn.execute(\n            f\"INSERT INTO pages ({_PAGE_COLUMNS}) \"\n            f\"SELECT {_PAGE_COLUMNS} FROM pages_legacy\"\n        )\n        conn.execute(\"DROP TABLE pages_legacy\")\n        conn.execute(\"COMMIT\")\n    except Exception:\n        conn.execute(\"ROLLBACK\")\n        raise\n    finally:\n        conn.isolation_level = previous_isolation_level\n\n\ndef get_connection(path: Path | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the schema and, only the first time the `pages` table is\n    created, seed the starting Pages. Pages are freely creatable/deletable\n    (see PAGE_SLUGS' docstring) and `init_db` runs again on every `cms serve`\n    restart, so this keys off whether the table already existed — not\n    whether it's currently empty — or deleting every page would make the\n    next restart look identical to a brand-new database and recreate them.\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = get_connection(path)\n    try:\n        existing_pages_row = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'pages'\"\n        ).fetchone()\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        _migrate_legacy_page_slug_check(conn, existing_pages_row[0] if existing_pages_row else None)\n        if existing_pages_row is None:\n            for slug in PAGE_SLUGS:\n                conn.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO pages (slug, title) VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                    (slug, PAGE_TITLES[slug]),\n                )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef create_user(email: str, password: str, role: str, *, active: bool = True) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role, active) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (email, auth.hash_password(password), role, int(active)),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_user_by_email(email: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email,)\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_user_by_id(user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef set_active(email: str, *, active: bool) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\", (int(active), email)\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_page_by_slug(slug: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef list_pages() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages ORDER BY slug\").fetchall()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef update_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"UPDATE pages\n               SET title = ?, body_md = ?, status = ?, author_id = ?,\n                   updated_at = datetime('now')\n               WHERE slug = ?\"\"\",\n            (title, body_md, status, author_id, slug),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef slug_is_valid(slug: str) -> bool:\n    return re.fullmatch(SLUG_PATTERN, slug) is not None\n\n\ndef create_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Insert a new page. Returns False if the slug is already taken.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (slug, title, body_md, status, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (slug, title, body_md, status, author_id),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        return True\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        return False\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef delete_page(slug: str) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":46,"oldLines":6,"newStart":46,"newLines":11,"lines":[" "," SLUG_PATTERN = \"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\""," ","+# \"index\" is reserved: app/publish.py exports the \"home\" page to index.html,","+# so a page slugged \"index\" would silently overwrite the real homepage on","+# export.","+_RESERVED_SLUGS = frozenset({\"index\"})","+"," _PAGE_COLUMNS = \"id, slug, title, body_md, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at\""," "," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e13a52e0-dfa4-4db5-aba3-f53a1b8c6dd3","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"0c4cea58-d7a8-4306-a846-cb4bf12946ad","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:58:14.105Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/publish.py","oldString":"    if not any(p[\"slug\"] == HOME_SLUG for p in published):\n        (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\",\n            )\n        )","newString":"    if not any(p[\"slug\"] == HOME_SLUG for p in published):\n        (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\", nav_links=nav_links,\n            )\n        )","originalFile":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nrender_site() reads only published Pages from the database (see CONTEXT.md's\n\"Publish\" entry — published-in-the-CMS is not the same as exported) and\nwrites one file per page: the \"home\" slug becomes index.html, every other\npublished slug becomes \"<slug>.html\". Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"menu.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\nAll exported files live flat at the root of site/, so a single relative name\n(\"style.css\", \"index.html\", \"<slug>.html\") resolves correctly from every page\nwithout tracking directory depth.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import content, db, settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nheader nav ul { display: flex; gap: 1rem; list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0.5rem 0 0; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\nHOME_SLUG = \"home\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _out_name(slug: str) -> str:\n    return \"index.html\" if slug == HOME_SLUG else f\"{slug}.html\"\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    published = [p for p in db.list_pages() if p[\"status\"] == \"published\"]\n\n    # Home is the entry point, not a nav link itself (see CONTEXT.md's \"Pages\"\n    # entry); every other published page appears in the nav on every page.\n    nav_links = [\n        {\"href\": _out_name(p[\"slug\"]), \"title\": p[\"title\"]}\n        for p in published if p[\"slug\"] != HOME_SLUG\n    ]\n\n    template = env.get_template(\"public/page.html\")\n    for page in published:\n        (out / _out_name(page[\"slug\"])).write_text(\n            template.render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE,\n                page_title=page[\"title\"],\n                body_html=content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]),\n                css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\",\n                nav_links=nav_links,\n            )\n        )\n\n    if not any(p[\"slug\"] == HOME_SLUG for p in published):\n        (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n                home_path=\"index.html\",\n            )\n        )\n\n    return out\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":78,"oldLines":7,"newStart":78,"newLines":7,"lines":["         (out / \"index.html\").write_text(","             env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(","                 title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",","-                home_path=\"index.html\",","+                home_path=\"index.html\", nav_links=nav_links,","             )","         )"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1d336099-0781-4b73-8735-3d14ee7b3c63","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"2e3059fb-8c49-452d-9351-0e6e6a0252a0","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:58:19.164Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_publish.py","oldString":"def test_a_freshly_created_extra_page_is_reachable_once_published(tmp_path):","newString":"def test_a_page_slugged_index_is_rejected(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Regression: \"index\" would collide with home's exported index.html\n    and silently overwrite it (see app/db.py's _RESERVED_SLUGS).\"\"\"\n    manager = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = manager.get(\"/admin/pages/new\")\n    csrf = response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n    response = manager.post(\n        \"/admin/pages/new\",\n        data={\"slug\": \"index\", \"title\": \"Index\", \"body_md\": \"x\",\n              \"status\": \"published\", \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert db.get_page_by_slug(\"index\") is None\n\n\ndef test_draft_home_falls_back_to_placeholder_with_nav_intact(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Regression: when Home itself is a draft, the placeholder fallback\n    must still carry nav_links so Menu/Our Story stay reachable.\"\"\"\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    home_html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    hrefs = set(re.findall(r'href=\"([^\"]+)\"', home_html))\n    assert {\"menu.html\", \"our-story.html\"} <= hrefs\n\n\ndef test_a_freshly_created_extra_page_is_reachable_once_published(tmp_path):","originalFile":"\"\"\"T03: publish pipeline. Extends the T00 publish tests (tests/test_t00.py) to\ncover real Page content: draft exclusion, relative paths, and nav reachability.\n\nSeam 2 per spec #1: render_site() reads only status='published' rows and\nwrites site/ with one file per published Page. No test reaches into the\ndatabase to check anything a visitor couldn't observe by reading the\nexported HTML.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef _set_page(slug, *, status, body_md=None):\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=page[\"title\"],\n        body_md=body_md if body_md is not None else f\"Content for {slug}.\",\n        status=status,\n        author_id=None,\n    )\n\n\ndef _publish_all():\n    for slug in db.PAGE_SLUGS:\n        _set_page(slug, status=\"published\")\n\n\ndef test_home_is_rendered_from_real_page_content(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for home\" in html\n\n\ndef test_other_published_pages_are_written(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert \"Content for menu\" in (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Content for our-story\" in (out / \"our-story.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_draft_page_is_never_written_to_site(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\"home\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert \"Content for menu\" not in f.read_text()\n\n\ndef test_all_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        html = f.read_text()\n        assert 'href=\"/' not in html\n        assert 'src=\"/' not in html\n\n\ndef test_every_published_page_is_reachable_from_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / test_non_home_pages_link_back_to_home(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    menu_html = (out / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"index.html\"' in menu_html\n\n\ndef test_a_page_in_draft_is_excluded_but_others_still_publish(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Acceptance criterion: publishing with one page still in draft.\"\"\"\n    _set_page(\"home\", \"index.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"our-story.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_unpublishing_then_republishing_removes_it_from_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _publish_all()\n    out_dir = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    render_site(out_dir)\n    assert (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"draft\")\n    render_site(out_dir)\n\n    assert not (out_dir / \"menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_markdown_is_sanitized_in_the_export(tmp_path):\n    _set_page(\n        \"home\", status=\"published\",\n        body_md='<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=\"alert(2)\">Tacos',\n    )\n    _set_page(\"menu\", status=\"published\")\n    _set_page(\"our-story\", status=\"published\")\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    html = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    # The payload must not survive as a live tag/attribute. MarkdownIt is\n    # built with html=False (see app/content.py), so raw HTML in the source\n    # is escaped to inert text — \"<script\" and \"onerror=\" as literal,\n    # non-executable characters {"parentUuid":"f4e4de9f-ac74-4897-bc8f-cd5e0c03cdc5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"f5a600aa-3c47-4286-8fe2-3bacec293e0c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019czsVhpxXZ68fFDBop5JX3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env python3\n2\t\"\"\"SessionEnd hook: copy this session's transcript into docs/transcripts/.\n3\t\n4\tTranscripts are required evidence (rubric H2). Saving them automatically means\n5\tyou cannot forget — in MP1 nobody remembered. The name follows the required\n6\tpattern; set CMS_STUDENT=first-last in .env (or your shell) so it is right.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\timport os\n12\timport re\n13\timport shutil\n14\timport sys\n15\tfrom datetime import datetime\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef student_slug(project: Path) -> str:\n20\t    name = os.environ.get(\"CMS_STUDENT\")\n21\t    if not name:\n22\t        env = project / \".env\"\n23\t        if env.exists():\n24\t            match = re.search(r\"^CMS_STUDENT=(.+)$\", env.read_text(), re.M)\n25\t            name = match.group(1).strip() if match else None\n26\t    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z-]\", \"\", (name or \"your-name\").lower().replace(\" \", \"-\"))\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef main() -> None:\n30\t    event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n31\t    source = Path(event.get(\"transcript_path\", \"\"))\n32\t    if not source.exists():\n33\t        return\n34\t    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or \".\")\n35\t    out = project / \"docs\" / \"transcripts\"\n36\t    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n37\t    number = f\"{len(list(out.glob('*.md'))) + 1:02d}\"\n38\t    target = out / (f\"iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_{number}_\"\n39\t                    f\"{student_slug(project)}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.md\")\n40\t    if not target.exists():\n41\t        shutil.copy2(source, target)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n45\t    try:\n46\t        main()\n47\t    except Exception:\n48\t        pass\n49\t    sys.exit(0)\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"41b3ad71-5324-448b-b561-d35cec75bd78","timestamp":"2026-09-29T04:04:00.606Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"SessionEnd hook: copy this session's transcript into docs/transcripts/.\n\nTranscripts are required evidence (rubric H2). Saving them automatically means\nyou cannot forget — in MP1 nobody remembered. The name follows the required\npattern; set CMS_STUDENT=first-last in .env (or your shell) so it is right.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef student_slug(project: Path) -> str:\n    name = os.environ.get(\"CMS_STUDENT\")\n    if not name:\n        env = project / \".env\"\n        if env.exists():\n            match = re.search(r\"^CMS_STUDENT=(.+)$\", env.read_text(), re.M)\n            name = match.group(1).strip() if match else None\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z-]\", \"\", (name or \"your-name\").lower().replace(\" \", \"-\"))\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    event = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    source = Path(event.get(\"transcript_path\", \"\"))\n    if not source.exists():\n        return\n    project = Path(os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\") or event.get(\"cwd\") or \".\")\n    out = project / \"docs\" / \"transcripts\"\n    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    number = f\"{len(list(out.glob('*.md'))) + 1:02d}\"\n    target = out / (f\"iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_{number}_\"\n                    f\"{student_slug(project)}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.md\")\n    if not target.exists():\n        shutil.copy2(source, target)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        main()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    sys.exit(0)\n","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":50}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f4e4de9f-ac74-4897-bc8f-cd5e0c03cdc5","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nSeeded Demo Manager and Demo Editor accounts can log in and log out via a session\\ncookie; a wrong password is refused with a clear message and no session is created.\\nThe user schema records role (admin/editor) and an active/deactivated flag, so every\\nlater ticket has something to check authorization against.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] Demo Manager and Demo Editor (seeded via `scripts/seed_demo.py`, passwords read\\n      from `.env.example`) can each log in and reach a themed landing area.\\n- [ ] Logging out ends the session; the next request to an admin route redirects to\\n      login.\\n- [ ] A wrong password is rejected with a clear message; no session is created.\\n- [ ] Passwords are stored as argon2 hashes only; plaintext is never stored or logged.\\n- [ ] The login form carries and validates a CSRF token.\\n- [ ] The user schema includes a role (admin/editor) and an active/deactivated flag;\\n      login refuses a deactivated user even with the correct password.\\n- [ ] `tests/test_login.py` (new) covers login success, wrong password, logout-then-\\n      blocked, and deactivated-login-refused, using the existing `client` fixture.\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\nNone (can start immediately)\\n\",\"reactions\":{\"url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/2/reactions\",\"total_count\":0,\"+1\":0,\"-1\":0,\"laugh\":0,\"hooray\":0,\"confused\":0,\"heart\":0,\"rocket\":0,\"eyes\":0},\"timeline_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/2/timeline\",\"performed_via_github_app\":null,\"state_reason\":\"completed\",\"pinned_comment\":null},{\"url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4\",\"repository_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms\",\"labels_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/labels{/name}\",\"comments_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/comments\",\"events_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/events\",\"html_url\":\"https://github.com/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4\",\"id\":5624460641,\"node_id\":\"I_kwDOUxdEZc8AAAABTz51YQ\",\"number\":4,\"title\":\"T03: Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\n`cms publish` exports only published content from the CMS to static `site/` HTML\\nwith relative paths and working navigation, and `cms deploy` pushes it to\\n`gh-pages`, giving a real Pages URL that serves the Home page.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] `uv run cms publish` writes `site/` with Home (and any other published\\n      permanent pages) rendered from the database, replacing the current hard-coded\\n      empty-items placeholder.\\n- [ ] Every `href=`/`src=` in the exported HTML is relative; zero root-absolute\\n      paths.\\n- [ ] A page left in draft status is never written to `site/` — verified by\\n      publishing with one page still in draft.\\n- [ ] Every published page, including Home, is reachable by following links in the\\n      exported HTML — no orphan pages.\\n- [ ] `uv run cms deploy` pushes `site/` to the `gh-pages` branch; the Pages URL\\n      serves the Home page over HTTPS.\\n- [ ] `tests/test_publish.py` extends the existing T00 publish tests to cover\\n      draft-excluded-from-export, relative-paths, and nav-reachability against real\\n      Page content (not the placeholder).\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n- #3 (T02: Permanent pages) — needs real Page content and a status field to export\\n  and to test draft-exclusion against.\\n\",\"reactions\":{\"url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/reactions\",\"total_count\":0,\"+1\":0,\"-1\":0,\"laugh\":0,\"hooray\":0,\"confused\":0,\"heart\":0,\"rocket\":0,\"eyes\":0},\"timeline_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/timeline\",\"performed_via_github_app\":null,\"state_reason\":\"completed\",\"pinned_comment\":null}]","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"37cc385a-7ec6-4999-a9c3-12acd631f256","timestamp":"2026-09-29T04:04:44.342Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"T04: Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nSeeded Demo Manager and Demo Editor accounts can log in and log out via a session\\ncookie; a wrong password is refused with a clear message and no session is created.\\nThe user schema records role (admin/editor) and an active/deactivated flag, so every\\nlater ticket has something to check authorization against.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] Demo Manager and Demo Editor (seeded via `scripts/seed_demo.py`, passwords read\\n      from `.env.example`) can each log in and reach a themed landing area.\\n- [ ] Logging out ends the session; the next request to an admin route redirects to\\n      login.\\n- [ ] A wrong password is rejected with a clear message; no session is created.\\n- [ ] Passwords are stored as argon2 hashes only; plaintext is never stored or logged.\\n- [ ] The login form carries and validates a CSRF token.\\n- [ ] The user schema includes a role (admin/editor) and an active/deactivated flag;\\n      login refuses a deactivated user even with the correct password.\\n- [ ] `tests/test_login.py` (new) covers login success, wrong password, logout-then-\\n      blocked, and deactivated-login-refused, using the existing `client` fixture.\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\nNone (can start Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\n`cms publish` exports only published content from the CMS to static `site/` HTML\\nwith relative paths and working navigation, and `cms deploy` pushes it to\\n`gh-pages`, giving a real Pages URL that serves the Home page.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] `uv run cms publish` writes `site/` with Home (and any other published\\n      permanent pages) rendered from the database, replacing the current hard-coded\\n      empty-items placeholder.\\n- [ ] Every `href=`/`src=` in the exported HTML is relative; zero root-absolute\\n      paths.\\n- [ ] A page left in draft status is never written to `site/` — verified by\\n      publishing with one page still in draft.\\n- [ ] Every published page, including Home, is reachable by following links in the\\n      exported HTML — no orphan pages.\\n- [ ] `uv run cms deploy` pushes `site/` to the `gh-pages` branch; the Pages URL\\n      serves the Home page over HTTPS.\\n- [ ] `tests/test_publish.py` extends the existing T00 publish tests to cover\\n      draft-excluded-from-export, relative-paths, and nav-reachability against real\\n      Page content (not the placeholder).\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n- #3 (T02: Permanent pages) — needs real Page content and a status field to export\\n  and to test draft-exclusion against.\\n\",\"reactions\":{\"url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/reactions\",\"total_count\":0,\"+1\":0,\"-1\":0,\"laugh\":0,\"hooray\":0,\"confused\":0,\"heart\":0,\"rocket\":0,\"eyes\":0},\"timeline_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4/timeline\",\"performed_via_github_app\":null,\"state_reason\":\"completed\",\"pinned_comment\":null}]","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"edcead09-3a08-475d-b548-8a04d35e2637","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":2},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"algoal0507-crypto/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"macos"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1ae43699-15d2-453a-a2f1-1ce46c6761c4","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"0f3aface-1ae7-4a1d-b0eb-7b8ef86d6b46","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}
